#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+void advanceBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    const size_t n = positions.size();
+    const size_t localCount = bodies.size();
+
+    // SIMD lanes are independent target bodies. Each lane visits the sources in
+    // the original order, without reassociating its floating-point reduction.
+    constexpr size_t lanes = 4;
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; localCount - i >= lanes; i += lanes) {
+        double x[lanes], y[lanes], z[lanes];
+        double Fx[lanes] = {}, Fy[lanes] = {}, Fz[lanes] = {};
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.x;
+            y[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.z;
+        }
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
+                const double dx = positions[j].x - x[k];
+                const double dy = positions[j].y - y[k];
+                const double dz = positions[j].z - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
+            auto& body = bodies[i + k];
+            body.vel.x += DT * Fx[k];
+            body.vel.y += DT * Fy[k];
+            body.vel.z += DT * Fz[k];
+            body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
+            body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
+            body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+        }
+    }
+
+    // Handle a partial SIMD block without introducing dummy bodies.
+    for (; i < localCount; ++i) {
+        auto& body = bodies[i];
         double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
-        
+

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
import os, re, subprocess
serial = '/tmp/nbody_mpi_reference'
parallel = '/tmp/nbody_mpi_build/nbody'
env = dict(os.environ, OMPI_ALLOW_RUN_AS_ROOT='1', OMPI_ALLOW_RUN_AS_ROOT_CONFIRM='1')
for n, steps, ranks in [(0,0,1),(0,3,4),(1,4,4),(3,2,5),(4,3,1),(17,3,1),(17,3,2),(17,3,4),(127,5,3),(1024,10,4),(31,0,3),(31,-1,3)]:
    args = ['-n',str(n),'-s',str(steps),'-r','-v']
    ref = subprocess.run([serial,*args], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout
    run = subprocess.run(['mpirun','--oversubscribe','-np',str(ranks),parallel,*args],env=env,capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=30)
    strip = lambda s: re.sub(r'Simulation time: .*\\n','',s)
    match = strip(ref) == strip(run.stdout)
    print(f'n={n}, steps={steps}, ranks={ranks}: return={run.returncode}, exact_output={match}', flush=True)
    if not match or run.returncode:
        print(ref)
        print(run.stdout)
        print(run.stderr)
        raise SystemExit(1)

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <type_traits>
#include <vector>

#include <mpi.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

// MPI sends doubles, rather than raw object bytes, for portable communication.
static_assert(std::is_standard_layout_v<Vec3> && sizeof(Vec3) == 3 * sizeof(double));
static_assert(std::is_standard_layout_v<Body> && sizeof(Body) == 6 * sizeof(double));
static_assert(offsetof(Body, pos) == 0 && offsetof(Body, vel) == sizeof(Vec3));

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void advanceBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
    const size_t n = positions.size();
    const size_t localCount = bodies.size();

    // SIMD lanes are independent target bodies. Each lane visits the sources in
    // the original order, without reassociating its floating-point reduction.
    constexpr size_t lanes = 4;
    size_t i = 0;
    for (; localCount - i >= lanes; i += lanes) {
        double x[lanes], y[lanes], z[lanes];
        double Fx[lanes] = {}, Fy[lanes] = {}, Fz[lanes] = {};
        for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
            x[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.x;
            y[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.y;
            z[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.z;
        }
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
                const double dx = positions[j].x - x[k];
                const double dy = positions[j].y - y[k];
                const double dz = positions[j].z - z[k];
                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;

                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
            }
        }
        for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
            auto& body = bodies[i + k];
            body.vel.x += DT * Fx[k];
            body.vel.y += DT * Fy[k];
            body.vel.z += DT * Fz[k];
            body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
            body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
            body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
        }
    }

    // Handle a partial SIMD block without introducing dummy bodies.
    for (; i < localCount; ++i) {
        auto& body = bodies[i];
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;

        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = positions[j].x - body.pos.x;
            const double dy = positions[j].y - body.pos.y;
            const double dz = positions[j].z - body.pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        body.vel.x += DT * Fx;
        body.vel.y += DT * Fy;
        body.vel.z += DT * Fz;
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
    int rank = 0, ranks = 1;
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks);

    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);
            MPI_Finalize();
            return 0;
        } else {
            if (rank == 0) {
                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
                printUsage(argv[0]);
            }
            MPI_Finalize();
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (numBodies < 0) {
        if (rank == 0) fprintf(stderr, "Number of bodies must be nonnegative\n");
        MPI_Finalize();
        return 1;
    }

    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
        printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
        printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    }

    // Counts are in bodies, avoiding overflow from multiplying MPI's int counts
    // by the number of coordinates. Empty ranks participate in all collectives.
    std::vector<int> counts(ranks), offsets(ranks);
    for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
        counts[r] = numBodies / ranks + (r < numBodies % ranks ? 1 : 0);
        offsets[r] = r * (numBodies / ranks) + std::min(r, numBodies % ranks);
    }

    MPI_Datatype positionType, bodyType, bodyPositionType;
    MPI_Type_contiguous(3, MPI_DOUBLE, &positionType);
    MPI_Type_commit(&positionType);
    MPI_Type_contiguous(6, MPI_DOUBLE, &bodyType);
    MPI_Type_commit(&bodyType);
    // Send positions straight from local bodies, skipping their velocities.
    MPI_Type_create_resized(positionType, 0, sizeof(Body), &bodyPositionType);
    MPI_Type_commit(&bodyPositionType);

    std::vector<Body> bodies(counts[rank]);
    std::vector<Vec3> positions(numBodies);
    std::vector<Body> globalBodies;
    if (rank == 0) {
        globalBodies.resize(numBodies);
        randomizeBodies(globalBodies);
        for (int i = 0; i < numBodies; ++i) positions[i] = globalBodies[i].pos;
    }
    MPI_Scatterv(globalBodies.data(), counts.data(), offsets.data(), bodyType,
                 bodies.data(), counts[rank], bodyType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    MPI_Bcast(positions.data(), numBodies, positionType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    // A full state is needed only for the original output/validation routines.
    if (!printResults && !validate) std::vector<Body>().swap(globalBodies);

    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        advanceBodies(bodies, positions);
        // The last positions need no replication when there is no next step.
        if (step + 1 < numSteps) {
            MPI_Allgatherv(bodies.data(), counts[rank], bodyPositionType,
                           positions.data(), counts.data(), offsets.data(),
                           positionType, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        }
    }
    const double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
    double duration = 0.0;
    MPI_Reduce(&elapsed, &duration, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    if (rank == 0) printf("Simulation time: %lld ms\n", static_cast<long long>(duration * 1000.0));

    if (printResults || validate) {
        MPI_Gatherv(bodies.data(), counts[rank], bodyType, globalBodies.data(),
                    counts.data(), offsets.data(), bodyType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    }
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults && rank == 0) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(numBodies) * 6);
        for (const auto& body : globalBodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    int result = 0;
    if (validate && rank == 0) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(globalBodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(globalBodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            result = 1;
        }
    }
    
    MPI_Bcast(&result, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    MPI_Type_free(&bodyPositionType);
    MPI_Type_free(&bodyType);
    MPI_Type_free(&positionType);
    MPI_Finalize();
    return result;
}

target_link_libraries(nbody PRIVATE MPI::MPI_CXX m)

-void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {

+void advanceBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    const size_t n = positions.size();
+    const size_t localCount = bodies.size();
+
+    // SIMD lanes are independent target bodies. Each lane visits the sources in
+    // the original order, without reassociating its floating-point reduction.
+    constexpr size_t lanes = 4;
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; localCount - i >= lanes; i += lanes) {
+        double x[lanes], y[lanes], z[lanes];
+        double Fx[lanes] = {}, Fy[lanes] = {}, Fz[lanes] = {};
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.x;
+            y[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.z;
+        }
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
+                const double dx = positions[j].x - x[k];
+                const double dy = positions[j].y - y[k];
+                const double dz = positions[j].z - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
+            auto& body = bodies[i + k];
+            body.vel.x += DT * Fx[k];
+            body.vel.y += DT * Fy[k];
+            body.vel.z += DT * Fz[k];
+            body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
+            body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
+            body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+        }
+    }

+    for (; i < localCount; ++i) {
+        auto& body = bodies[i];

+void advanceBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    const size_t n = positions.size();
+    const size_t localCount = bodies.size();
+
+    // SIMD lanes are independent target bodies. Each lane visits the sources in
+    // the original order, without reassociating its floating-point reduction.
+    constexpr size_t lanes = 4;
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; localCount - i >= lanes; i += lanes) {
+        double x[lanes], y[lanes], z[lanes];
+        double Fx[lanes] = {}, Fy[lanes] = {}, Fz[lanes] = {};
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.x;
+            y[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.z;
+        }
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
+                const double dx = positions[j].x - x[k];
+                const double dy = positions[j].y - y[k];
+                const double dz = positions[j].z - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }

+            auto& body = bodies[i + k];
+            body.vel.x += DT * Fx[k];
+            body.vel.y += DT * Fy[k];

+void advanceBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    const size_t n = positions.size();
+    const size_t localCount = bodies.size();
+
+    // SIMD lanes are independent target bodies. Each lane visits the sources in
+    // the original order, without reassociating its floating-point reduction.
+    constexpr size_t lanes = 4;
+    size_t i = 0;
+    for (; localCount - i >= lanes; i += lanes) {
+        double x[lanes], y[lanes], z[lanes];
+        double Fx[lanes] = {}, Fy[lanes] = {}, Fz[lanes] = {};
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.x;
+            y[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.z;
+        }
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
+                const double dx = positions[j].x - x[k];
+                const double dy = positions[j].y - y[k];
+                const double dz = positions[j].z - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
+            auto& body = bodies[i + k];
+            body.vel.x += DT * Fx[k];
+            body.vel.y += DT * Fy[k];
+            body.vel.z += DT * Fz[k];
+            body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
+            body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
+            body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
+        }
+    }

+    for (; i < localCount; ++i) {
+        auto& body = bodies[i];

+void advanceBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    const size_t n = positions.size();
+    const size_t localCount = bodies.size();
+
+    // SIMD lanes are independent target bodies. Each lane visits the sources in
+    // the original order, without reassociating its floating-point reduction.
+    constexpr size_t lanes = 4;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < localCount; i += lanes) {
+        const size_t active = std::min(lanes, localCount - i);
+        double x[lanes] = {}, y[lanes] = {}, z[lanes] = {};
+        double Fx[lanes] = {}, Fy[lanes] = {}, Fz[lanes] = {};
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.x;
+            y[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.z;
+        }

-            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;

-            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-

-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;

+                const double dx = positions[j].x - x[k];
+                const double dy = positions[j].y - y[k];
+                const double dz = positions[j].z - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        // Unused target lanes in the last block are never stored. Using the
+        // same kernel for the tail keeps rounding independent of rank counts.
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            auto& body = bodies[i + k];
+            body.vel.x += DT * Fx[k];
+            body.vel.y += DT * Fy[k];
+            body.vel.z += DT * Fz[k];

The first timing check shows good scaling: for 8,192 bodies over 5 steps, the serial code took 2,487 ms and the MPI version took 85 ms on 8 ranks. Correctness checks caught a small rounding difference in partial SIMD blocks; I’m adjusting that path to keep results independent of the rank count.

+void advanceBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    const size_t n = positions.size();

+    // the original order, without reassociating its floating-point reduction.
+    constexpr size_t lanes = 4;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < localCount; i += lanes) {
+        const size_t active = std::min(lanes, localCount - i);
+        double x[lanes] = {}, y[lanes] = {}, z[lanes] = {};
+        double Fx[lanes] = {}, Fy[lanes] = {}, Fz[lanes] = {};

+                const double dy = positions[j].y - y[k];
+                const double dz = positions[j].z - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        // Unused target lanes in the last block are never stored. Using the
+        // same kernel for the tail keeps rounding independent of rank counts.
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {

+            body.vel.x += DT * Fx[k];

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
source = Path('nbody/nbody.cpp').read_text()
old = '''        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
            auto& body = bodies[i + k];
            body.vel.x += DT * Fx[k];
            body.vel.y += DT * Fy[k];
            body.vel.z += DT * Fz[k];'''
new = '''        for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
            Fx[k] *= DT;
            Fy[k] *= DT;
            Fz[k] *= DT;
        }
        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
            auto& body = bodies[i + k];
            body.vel.x += Fx[k];
            body.vel.y += Fy[k];
            body.vel.z += Fz[k];'''
assert old in source
command = ['mpicxx','-x','c++','-','-I'+str(Path('nbody').resolve()),'-std=c++20','-O3','-march=native','-fno-math-errno','-DOMPI_SKIP_MPICXX','-DMPICH_SKIP_MPICXX','-o','/tmp/nbody_mpi_trial']
subprocess.run(command,input=source.replace(old,new),text=True,check=True)
for n in [1,4,17,127,1024]:
    args = ['-n',str(n),'-s','3','-r','-v']
    ref = subprocess.check_output(['/tmp/nbody_mpi_reference',*args],text=True)
    run = subprocess.check_output(['/tmp/nbody_mpi_trial',*args],text=True)
    ref = '\\n'.join(l for l in ref.splitlines() if not l.startswith('Simulation time:'))

+void advanceBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    const size_t n = positions.size();
+    const size_t localCount = bodies.size();
+
+    // SIMD lanes are independent target bodies. Each lane visits the sources in
+    // the original order, without reassociating its floating-point reduction.
+    constexpr size_t lanes = 4;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < localCount; i += lanes) {
+        const size_t active = std::min(lanes, localCount - i);
+        double x[lanes] = {}, y[lanes] = {}, z[lanes] = {};
+        double Fx[lanes] = {}, Fy[lanes] = {}, Fz[lanes] = {};
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.x;
+            y[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.z;
+        }

-            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
-            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
-            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
-            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
-            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
-            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
+                const double dx = positions[j].x - x[k];
+                const double dy = positions[j].y - y[k];
+                const double dz = positions[j].z - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        // Unused target lanes in the last block are never stored. Using the
+        // same kernel for the tail keeps rounding independent of rank counts.
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            auto& body = bodies[i + k];
+            body.vel.x += DT * Fx[k];
+            body.vel.y += DT * Fy[k];
+            body.vel.z += DT * Fz[k];
+            body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
+            body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
+            body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
source = Path('nbody/nbody.cpp').read_text()
begin = source.index('void advanceBodies(')
end = source.index('\\ndouble computeTotalEnergy',begin)
block = '''constexpr size_t FORCE_LANES = 4;

// Keeping full and partial blocks in one kernel makes rounding independent of
// the decomposition, while vectorizing across independent target bodies.
[[gnu::noinline]] void advanceBlock(Body* bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
    double x[FORCE_LANES], y[FORCE_LANES], z[FORCE_LANES];
    double Fx[FORCE_LANES] = {}, Fy[FORCE_LANES] = {}, Fz[FORCE_LANES] = {};
    for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_LANES; ++k) {
        x[k] = bodies[k].pos.x;
        y[k] = bodies[k].pos.y;
        z[k] = bodies[k].pos.z;
    }
    for (size_t j = 0; j < positions.size(); ++j) {
        for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_LANES; ++k) {
            const double dx = positions[j].x - x[k];
            const double dy = positions[j].y - y[k];
            const double dz = positions[j].z - z[k];
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
            Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
            Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
        }
    }
    for (size_t k = 0; k < FORCE_LANES; ++k) {
        auto& body = bodies[k];
        body.vel.x += DT * Fx[k];
        body.vel.y += DT * Fy[k];
        body.vel.z += DT * Fz[k];
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

void advanceBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
    size_t i = 0;
    for (; bodies.size() - i >= FORCE_LANES; i += FORCE_LANES) {
        advanceBlock(bodies.data() + i, positions);
    }
    const size_t remaining = bodies.size() - i;
    if (remaining "'!= 0) {
        Body tail[FORCE_LANES] = {};
        std::copy_n(bodies.data() + i, remaining, tail);
        advanceBlock(tail, positions);
        std::copy_n(tail, remaining, bodies.data() + i);
    }
}
'"'''
command = ['mpicxx','-x','c++','-','-I'+str(Path('nbody').resolve()),'-std=c++20','-O3','-march=native','-fno-math-errno','-DOMPI_SKIP_MPICXX','-DMPICH_SKIP_MPICXX','-o','/tmp/nbody_mpi_trial']
subprocess.run(command,input=source[:begin]+block+source[end:],text=True,check=True)
for n in [1,4,17,127,1024]:
    args = ['-n',str(n),'-s','3','-r','-v']
    ref = subprocess.check_output(['/tmp/nbody_mpi_reference',*args],text=True)
    run = subprocess.check_output(['/tmp/nbody_mpi_trial',*args],text=True)
    ref = '\\n'.join(l for l in ref.splitlines() if not l.startswith('Simulation time:'))
    run = '\\n'.join(l for l in run.splitlines() if not l.startswith('Simulation time:'))
    print(n,ref==run,flush=True)

            body.vel.z += DT * Fz[k];'''

            volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
            volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];
            body.vel.x += dvx;
            body.vel.y += dvy;

+void advanceBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    const size_t n = positions.size();
+    const size_t localCount = bodies.size();
+
+    // SIMD lanes are independent target bodies. Each lane visits the sources in
+    // the original order, without reassociating its floating-point reduction.
+    constexpr size_t lanes = 4;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < localCount; i += lanes) {
+        const size_t active = std::min(lanes, localCount - i);
+        double x[lanes] = {}, y[lanes] = {}, z[lanes] = {};
+        double Fx[lanes] = {}, Fy[lanes] = {}, Fz[lanes] = {};
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.x;
+            y[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.z;
+        }
         for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
-            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
-            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
-            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
-            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
-            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
-            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
+                const double dx = positions[j].x - x[k];
+                const double dy = positions[j].y - y[k];
+                const double dz = positions[j].z - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        // Unused target lanes in the last block are never stored. Using the
+        // same kernel for the tail keeps rounding independent of rank counts.
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            auto& body = bodies[i + k];
+            body.vel.x += DT * Fx[k];
+            body.vel.y += DT * Fy[k];
+            body.vel.z += DT * Fz[k];
+            body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
+            body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
+            body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
         }

+void advanceBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    const size_t n = positions.size();
+    const size_t localCount = bodies.size();
+
+    // SIMD lanes are independent target bodies. Each lane visits the sources in
+    // the original order, without reassociating its floating-point reduction.
+    constexpr size_t lanes = 4;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < localCount; i += lanes) {
+        const size_t active = std::min(lanes, localCount - i);
+        double x[lanes] = {}, y[lanes] = {}, z[lanes] = {};
+        double Fx[lanes] = {}, Fy[lanes] = {}, Fz[lanes] = {};
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.x;
+            y[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.z;
+        }
         for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
-            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
-            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
-            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
-            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
-            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
-            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
+                const double dx = positions[j].x - x[k];
+                const double dy = positions[j].y - y[k];
+                const double dz = positions[j].z - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        // Unused target lanes in the last block are never stored. Using the
+        // same kernel for the tail keeps rounding independent of rank counts.
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            auto& body = bodies[i + k];
+            body.vel.x += DT * Fx[k];
+            body.vel.y += DT * Fy[k];
+            body.vel.z += DT * Fz[k];
+            body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
+            body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
+            body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;

-void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {

+void advanceBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    const size_t n = positions.size();
+    const size_t localCount = bodies.size();
+
+    // SIMD lanes are independent target bodies. Each lane visits the sources in
+    // the original order, without reassociating its floating-point reduction.
+    constexpr size_t lanes = 4;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < localCount; i += lanes) {
+        const size_t active = std::min(lanes, localCount - i);
+        double x[lanes] = {}, y[lanes] = {}, z[lanes] = {};
+        double Fx[lanes] = {}, Fy[lanes] = {}, Fz[lanes] = {};
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.x;
+            y[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.z;

-            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;

-            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;

+                const double dx = positions[j].x - x[k];
+                const double dy = positions[j].y - y[k];
+                const double dz = positions[j].z - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        // Unused target lanes in the last block are never stored. Using the
+        // same kernel for the tail keeps rounding independent of rank counts.
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            auto& body = bodies[i + k];
+            body.vel.x += DT * Fx[k];
+            body.vel.y += DT * Fy[k];
+            body.vel.z += DT * Fz[k];
+            body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
+            body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
+            body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
         }

+void advanceBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {

+    const size_t localCount = bodies.size();
+
+    // SIMD lanes are independent target bodies. Each lane visits the sources in
+    // the original order, without reassociating its floating-point reduction.
+    constexpr size_t lanes = 4;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < localCount; i += lanes) {
+        const size_t active = std::min(lanes, localCount - i);
+        double x[lanes] = {}, y[lanes] = {}, z[lanes] = {};
+        double Fx[lanes] = {}, Fy[lanes] = {}, Fz[lanes] = {};
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.x;

+            z[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.z;

+#endif

+                const double dx = positions[j].x - x[k];
+                const double dy = positions[j].y - y[k];
+                const double dz = positions[j].z - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }

+        // same kernel for the tail keeps rounding independent of rank counts.
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            auto& body = bodies[i + k];
+            // Round the velocity increment before adding, as in the original
+            // force pass. These O(n) stores prevent contraction with velocity.
+            const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];
+            const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
+            const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];
+            body.vel.x += dvx;
+            body.vel.y += dvy;
+            body.vel.z += dvz;
+            body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;

+            body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;

The rounding fix now matches the serial output exactly in the cases checked, including hashes and final energy. It also retains the SIMD speedup. I’m checking uneven partitions, ranks with no bodies, and command-line behavior before a final scaling run.

    if clean(ref.stdout) "'!= clean(run.stdout) or ref.returncode != run.returncode:

+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;

+        // same kernel for the tail keeps rounding independent of rank counts.
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {

+            // Round the velocity increment before adding, as in the original
+            // force pass. These O(n) stores prevent contraction with velocity.
+            const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];
+            const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
+            const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];
+            body.vel.x += dvx;
+            body.vel.y += dvy;
+            body.vel.z += dvz;

+void advanceBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    const size_t n = positions.size();
+    const size_t localCount = bodies.size();
+
+    // SIMD lanes are independent target bodies. Each lane visits the sources in
+    // the original order, without reassociating its floating-point reduction.
+    constexpr size_t lanes = 4;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < localCount; i += lanes) {
+        const size_t active = std::min(lanes, localCount - i);
+        double x[lanes] = {}, y[lanes] = {}, z[lanes] = {};
+        double Fx[lanes] = {}, Fy[lanes] = {}, Fz[lanes] = {};
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.x;
+            y[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.z;
+        }
         for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
-            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
-            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
-            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
-            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
-            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
-            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+            // Vectorize targets instead of unrolling this loop into separate
+            // distance calculations for each force component.
+#if defined(__GNUC__)
+#pragma GCC unroll 1
+#endif
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
+                const double dx = positions[j].x - x[k];
+                const double dy = positions[j].y - y[k];
+                const double dz = positions[j].z - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        // Unused target lanes in the last block are never stored. Using the
+        // same kernel for the tail keeps rounding independent of rank counts.
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            auto& body = bodies[i + k];
+            // Round the velocity increment before adding, as in the original
+            // force pass. These O(n) stores prevent contraction with velocity.
+            const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];
+            const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
+            const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];

+            body.vel.z += dvz;

    93	        }
    94	    }
    95	}
    96	
    97	double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    98	    double energy = 0.0;
    99	    const size_t n = bodies.size();
   100	    
   101	    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
   102	    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
   103	        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
   104	                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
   105	                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
   106	    }
   107	    
   108	    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
   109	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   110	        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
   215	    MPI_Type_commit(&positionType);
   216	    MPI_Type_contiguous(6, MPI_DOUBLE, &bodyType);
   217	    MPI_Type_commit(&bodyType);
   218	    // Send positions straight from local bodies, skipping their velocities.
   219	    MPI_Type_create_resized(positionType, 0, sizeof(Body), &bodyPositionType);
   220	    MPI_Type_commit(&bodyPositionType);
   221	
   222	    std::vector<Body> bodies(counts[rank]);
   223	    std::vector<Vec3> positions(numBodies);
   224	    std::vector<Body> globalBodies;
   225	    if (rank == 0) {
   226	        globalBodies.resize(numBodies);
   227	        randomizeBodies(globalBodies);
   228	        for (int i = 0; i < numBodies; ++i) positions[i] = globalBodies[i].pos;
   229	    }
   230	    MPI_Scatterv(globalBodies.data(), counts.data(), offsets.data(), bodyType,
   231	                 bodies.data(), counts[rank], bodyType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   232	    MPI_Bcast(positions.data(), numBodies, positionType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   233	    // A full state is needed only for the original output/validation routines.
   234	    if (!printResults && !validate) std::vector<Body>().swap(globalBodies);
   235	
   236	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   237	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   238	    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
   239	        advanceBodies(bodies, positions);
   240	        // The last positions need no replication when there is no next step.
   241	        if (step + 1 < numSteps) {
   242	            MPI_Allgatherv(bodies.data(), counts[rank], bodyPositionType,
   243	                           positions.data(), counts.data(), offsets.data(),
   244	                           positionType, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   245	        }
   246	    }
   247	    const double elapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   248	    double duration = 0.0;
   249	    MPI_Reduce(&elapsed, &duration, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   250	    if (rank == 0) printf("Simulation time: %lld ms\n", static_cast<long long>(duration * 1000.0));
   251	
   252	    if (printResults || validate) {
   253	        MPI_Gatherv(bodies.data(), counts[rank], bodyType, globalBodies.data(),
   254	                    counts.data(), offsets.data(), bodyType, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   255	    }
   256	    
   257	    // Print results for external validation
   258	    if (printResults && rank == 0) {
   259	        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
   260	        std::vector<double> bodyData;
   261	        bodyData.reserve(static_cast<size_t>(numBodies) * 6);
   262	        for (const auto& body : globalBodies) {
   263	            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
   264	            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
   265	            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
   266	            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
   267	            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
   268	            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
   269	        }
   270	        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
   271	    }
   272	    
   273	    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
   274	    int result = 0;
   275	    if (validate && rank == 0) {
   276	        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
   277	        
   278	        if (validateSimulation(globalBodies)) {
   279	            // Report final energy for reference
   280	            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(globalBodies);
   281	            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
   282	            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   283	        } else {
   284	            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   285	            result = 1;
   286	        }
   287	    }
   288	    
   289	    MPI_Bcast(&result, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   290	    MPI_Type_free(&bodyPositionType);
   291	    MPI_Type_free(&bodyType);
   292	    MPI_Type_free(&positionType);
   293	    MPI_Finalize();
   294	    return result;
   295	}

+void advanceBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {

+    // SIMD lanes are independent target bodies. Each lane visits the sources in
+    // the original order, without reassociating its floating-point reduction.
+    constexpr size_t lanes = 4;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < localCount; i += lanes) {
+        const size_t active = std::min(lanes, localCount - i);
+        double x[lanes] = {}, y[lanes] = {}, z[lanes] = {};
+        double Fx[lanes] = {}, Fy[lanes] = {}, Fz[lanes] = {};

-            Fy += dy * invDist3;

+#endif

+                const double dy = positions[j].y - y[k];
+                const double dz = positions[j].z - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;

+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }

+        // same kernel for the tail keeps rounding independent of rank counts.
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {

+            // Round the velocity increment before adding, as in the original
+            // force pass. These O(n) stores prevent contraction with velocity.
+            const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];

+        double Fx[lanes] = {}, Fy[lanes] = {}, Fz[lanes] = {};
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.x;
+            y[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.z;
+        }
         for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
-            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
-            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
-            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
-            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
-            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
-            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+            // Vectorize targets instead of unrolling this loop into separate
+            // distance calculations for each force component.
+#if defined(__GNUC__)
+#pragma GCC unroll 1
+#endif
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
+                const double dx = positions[j].x - x[k];
+                const double dy = positions[j].y - y[k];
+                const double dz = positions[j].z - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        // Unused target lanes in the last block are never stored. Using the
+        // same kernel for the tail keeps rounding independent of rank counts.
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            auto& body = bodies[i + k];
+            // Round the velocity increment before adding, as in the original
+            // force pass. These O(n) stores prevent contraction with velocity.
+            const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];
+            const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
+            const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];
+            body.vel.x += dvx;
+            body.vel.y += dvy;
+            body.vel.z += dvz;
+            body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
+            body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
+            body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;

-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;

+void advanceBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    const size_t n = positions.size();
+    const size_t localCount = bodies.size();
+
+    // SIMD lanes are independent target bodies. Each lane visits the sources in
+    // the original order, without reassociating its floating-point reduction.
+    constexpr size_t lanes = 4;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < localCount; i += lanes) {
+        const size_t active = std::min(lanes, localCount - i);
+        double x[lanes] = {}, y[lanes] = {}, z[lanes] = {};
+        double Fx[lanes] = {}, Fy[lanes] = {}, Fz[lanes] = {};
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.x;
+            y[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.z;
+        }
         for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
-            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
-            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
-            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
-            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
-            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
-            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+            // Vectorize targets instead of unrolling this loop into separate
+            // distance calculations for each force component.
+#if defined(__GNUC__)
+#pragma GCC unroll 1
+#endif
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
+                const double dx = positions[j].x - x[k];
+                const double dy = positions[j].y - y[k];
+                const double dz = positions[j].z - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        // Unused target lanes in the last block are never stored. Using the
+        // same kernel for the tail keeps rounding independent of rank counts.
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            auto& body = bodies[i + k];
+            // Round the velocity increment before adding, as in the original
+            // force pass. These O(n) stores prevent contraction with velocity.
+            const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];
+            const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
+            const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];

+            body.vel.y += dvy;
+            body.vel.z += dvz;

+void advanceBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    const size_t n = positions.size();
+    const size_t localCount = bodies.size();
+
+    // SIMD lanes are independent target bodies. Each lane visits the sources in
+    // the original order, without reassociating its floating-point reduction.
+    constexpr size_t lanes = 4;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < localCount; i += lanes) {
+        const size_t active = std::min(lanes, localCount - i);
+        double x[lanes] = {}, y[lanes] = {}, z[lanes] = {};
+        double Fx[lanes] = {}, Fy[lanes] = {}, Fz[lanes] = {};
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.x;
+            y[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.z;

-            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;

-            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;

-            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+            // Vectorize targets instead of unrolling this loop into separate
+            // distance calculations for each force component.

+#pragma GCC unroll 1
+#endif
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
+                const double dx = positions[j].x - x[k];
+                const double dy = positions[j].y - y[k];
+                const double dz = positions[j].z - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        // Unused target lanes in the last block are never stored. Using the
+        // same kernel for the tail keeps rounding independent of rank counts.
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            auto& body = bodies[i + k];
+            // Round the velocity increment before adding, as in the original
+            // force pass. These O(n) stores prevent contraction with velocity.
+            const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];

codex
Implemented unconditional MPI parallelism with balanced body partitions, position-only exchanges, and SIMD force computation. CMake now requires MPI; the executable remains `nbody`. Only the two existing files were changed.

All 21 checks passed, including exact serial hashes, uneven partitions, and empty ranks.

Local benchmark, 8,192 bodies over 5 steps: **2,477 ms serial → 80 ms with 8 MPI ranks**, about **31× faster**.

+void advanceBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<Vec3>& positions) {
+    const size_t n = positions.size();
+    const size_t localCount = bodies.size();
+
+    // SIMD lanes are independent target bodies. Each lane visits the sources in
+    // the original order, without reassociating its floating-point reduction.
+    constexpr size_t lanes = 4;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < localCount; i += lanes) {
+        const size_t active = std::min(lanes, localCount - i);
+        double x[lanes] = {}, y[lanes] = {}, z[lanes] = {};
+        double Fx[lanes] = {}, Fy[lanes] = {}, Fz[lanes] = {};
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            x[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.x;
+            y[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.y;
+            z[k] = bodies[i + k].pos.z;
+        }
         for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
-            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
-            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
-            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
-            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
-            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
-            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
-            
-            Fx += dx * invDist3;
-            Fy += dy * invDist3;
-            Fz += dz * invDist3;
+            // Vectorize targets instead of unrolling this loop into separate
+            // distance calculations for each force component.
+#if defined(__GNUC__)
+#pragma GCC unroll 1
+#endif
+            for (size_t k = 0; k < lanes; ++k) {
+                const double dx = positions[j].x - x[k];
+                const double dy = positions[j].y - y[k];
+                const double dz = positions[j].z - z[k];
+                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
+                const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
+                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
+
+                Fx[k] += dx * invDist3;
+                Fy[k] += dy * invDist3;
+                Fz[k] += dz * invDist3;
+            }
+        }
+        // Unused target lanes in the last block are never stored. Using the
+        // same kernel for the tail keeps rounding independent of rank counts.
+        for (size_t k = 0; k < active; ++k) {
+            auto& body = bodies[i + k];
+            // Round the velocity increment before adding, as in the original
+            // force pass. These O(n) stores prevent contraction with velocity.
+            const volatile double dvx = DT * Fx[k];
+            const volatile double dvy = DT * Fy[k];
+            const volatile double dvz = DT * Fz[k];
+            body.vel.x += dvx;
+            body.vel.y += dvy;
+            body.vel.z += dvz;
+            body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
+            body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
+            body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
         }
-        
-        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
-        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
-        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
     }
 }
